# A2/A3 Vector Builtin Inventory

## 1. Collection Constraints

- Collection root: `.local/cann`, which is a symlink to `/home/mouliangyu/.local/ascend/beta.1/cann` in this workspace.
- Architecture scope: A2/A3 vector builtins whose callable C++ form uses memory pointers, not the A5-style `vector_*` value operands.
- C++ signature source: `.local/cann/tools/tikicpulib/lib/include/stub_fun.h`, filtered to declarations with pointer operands and without any `vector_*` parameter.
- Builtin-name mapping source: `.local/cann/x86_64-linux/ccec_compiler/lib/clang/15.0.5/include/cce_aicore_intrinsics.h` and `cce_aicore_intrinsics_3101.h`, using `clang_builtin_alias(__builtin_cce_...)` declarations.
- Included signatures must satisfy all of these conditions: function name is a vector builtin wrapper such as `v*` or `vector_dup`; at least one operand is a pointer, usually `__ubuf__ T*`; no operand uses the A5/new-vector `vector_*` types; and the wrapper can be mapped to a `__builtin_cce_*` alias in the CCE headers.
- Excluded entries include all `__clang_cce_vector_intrinsics.h` vector-value builtins, all `vector_*` input/output declarations from `stub_fun.h`, high-level AscendC wrappers, non-vector scalar/system builtins, x86/standard Clang builtins, OPP operator implementations, and names without a complete pointer-form signature in the scanned files.
- Overloads are preserved because the same builtin name can have different pointer element types, scalar operands, packed `uint64_t config` forms, and expanded repeat/stride forms.
- LLVM IR signatures were probed by compiling one representative pointer-form overload for each builtin with `.local/cann/x86_64-linux/bin/ccec -c -save-temps -xcce` and disassembling the emitted device bitcode with LLVM 15 `llvm-dis`.
- Probe targets were tried in this order until a `llvm.hivm.*` declaration was captured: `dav-m200-vec`, `dav-m200`, `dav-m300`, `dav-c220-vec`, `dav-c310-vec`, `dav-m210-vec`, `dav-l300-vec`, `dav-l300`, `dav-t300`, `dav-t200`, `dav-t210`, `dav-l310`, `dav-l311`, `dav-l311-eff`, `dav-l510`, `dav-s200`, `dav-c220`, `dav-c310`, `dav-m310`, `dav-m201`, `dav-n350`, `dav-920r1-vec`, `dav-920r1`, `dav-510r2`, `dav-380r1-cube`, `dav-c100`, `dav-l100`, `dav-l100-es`, `dav-l200`, `dav-l210`, `dav-m100`, `dav-s200-es`, `dav-t100`, `dav-t100-es`.
- LLVM IR declaration coverage: 200 of 200 builtin names captured a complete `declare @llvm.hivm.*` signature. The IR signature is target- and overload-dependent; the listed declaration is the observed lowering for the representative pointer-form overload shown under `Probe C++ signature`.
- Inventory count: 200 unique builtin names and 939 complete pointer-form overload signatures.
